Turkey to the chairman of the Grand National Assembly The consumption of olives and olive oil is also increasing in parallel with healthy and balanced diets. It is known that 90 percent of the world's olive farming is done in the Mediterranean basin and the rest in Latin America. Important olive producer countries are Spain, Italy, Greece, Tunisia, Syria and Turkey, respectively. The world's olive oil is about 750-800,000 tons, while the table olives are about 700,50,000 tonnes of exports. Studies indicate that there are some 320 thousand olive family businesses in our country. During the 2001-2002 season in Turkey, our olive obelisks were constantly increased, reaching 813,765 hectares during the 2012-2013 season. According to statistics, with 101,600,000 olive trees in 2002, our olive tree presence today reached 168 million. In the 2015-2016 production season, the yield will be 7.6 kg per tree, according to the National Register Committee of Olive Oil and Olive Oil Recolte. It was estimated that the 2015-2016 season would produce about 18,000 tonnes of olives and 143,000 tonnes of olive oil. Although the number of modern olive groves has shown an increased trend in recent years, they are not at the desired point. In economic and social terms, our important sector for our country, oliverism, has a number of problems that are looking for a solution. In our traditionally traditional way of olivery, efficiency is very low for modern-day olive-making countries. For example, in Spain and Italy, the yield per tree is between 45 and 150 kilograms, and in our country, it weighs between 9 and 14 kg. Efficiency is the most important problem of our olivery. There has been no similar increase in production of olives and olive oil, despite serious increase in the number of trees since the 2000s. Natural oil production in our country is 25 to 30 per cent of our total production, while refined oil production is 70-75 per cent. This has a negative effect on our export of olive oil. In other manufacturer countries, natural oil production is 80-90 percent. Most of our exports to the EU are exported because the EU imposes high customs tax on packaging exports. Turkey's packaged oil exports are at a level of one-third of total exports. In our country, the consumption of olive oil per capita is known to be around two liters. The number is 24 litres in Greece, the other oil producer countries, Italy and Spain, 14 litres in Tunisia, Portugal, Lebanon and Syria. In 2008, a Parliamentary Research Commission was established at the TBM to determine the measures needed to be taken by the production of olives and olive oil and other herbal oils and trade, and the Commission developed very valuable suggestions by doing its necessary work. However, the Parliamentary Commission for Research reports have not been submitted to implementation despite the long-term period of time. As part of our new circumstances and the point of our olivery, the matter will be re-examined and the necessary work will have significant benefits. We propose and propose that a parliamentary investigation of the Constitution shall be opened under Articles 104 and 105 of the Constitution, under Articles 104 and 105 of the Constitution, in order to determine the problems in our olives and quality and to improve the foreign trade opportunities of our oliveism, to identify organisation and marketing issues and to develop solution proposals. In the past two years, the living conditions of asylum seekers from Syria have not been corrected at the desired rate. Many of the millions of refugees in our country are leaving camps to ensure their own living conditions, and then facing the worst crimes of humanity, such as human trafficking, while trying to defect to European countries. With the hope of returning to their country's political climate declines, asylum seekers must adapt to society in almost all areas, including housing in Turkey, work, education, and health. Apart from those registered in our country, there are many refugees who are not registered. The asylum seekers are able to make transactions in Turkey with documents given to them and substitute for identity, and are able to benefit from such opportunities as education and health, despite limited possibilities. However, it is not possible for asylum seekers who are not registered with their crossing the border to benefit from such opportunities. The asylum seekers need to work in order to survive. However, they are subjected to economic abuse by taking advantage of their difficult circumstances. In other words, they work at low prices and unregistered. It presents a negative picture of both the safety of working life and the human condition of asylum seekers, and the exploitation of labor is involved. Refugees who leave the camps and try to survive their own lives are struggling and fighting against cold weather conditions in the streets, under bridges and stairs. This poses a serious threat to both personal and social health. Human Rights Watch reported that in 2014-2015, there were 708,000 Syrian refugee children, and more than 400,000 of them didn't go to school. The report stressed that only 25 per cent of Syrian children living outside the camps were enrolled in school, although the school registration rates reached 90 per cent in refugee camps. Syrian refugees who have been heavily coming to Turkey for the past three years have been trying to go to Europe in parallel with the lack of regular life in our country, but European countries have been trying to avoid facing the consequences of this wound in the Middle East with their own responsibility and to leave Turkey alone. Hundreds of people try to cross the border and die in the Aegean Sea, while Turkey begins the day with lifeless bodies washed ashore every morning. This situation is being monitored and accepted by all responsible people. Today, those who fueled the civil war over Syria to achieve their goals, shed false tears for Aylan, for little Sena, but refrain from stepping into the world's harmony with the refugees in human conditions. The EU-Turkey Summit, which took place on November 29, 2015, revealed an embarrassing outcome for humanity, as well as negotiating the living conditions of Syrian refugees, as reflected in public opinion. This agreement also led to assessments that Turkey had been bribed in exchange for a buffer state at the point of preventing refugees from moving to Europe. Not only Turkey or Syrian refugees, but the policies Turkey will implement on national and international platforms in order to develop solutions by identifying these problems that could be seen throughout the world for the next hundred years, and to harmonise with the community must be determined. Turkey to the chairman of the Grand National Assembly Turkey has been an importer of 73.5 per cent of its energy consumption as a result of policies that have been wrongly and mainly preempted commercial interest over the years. In 1990, our external dependency rate increased to 67 per cent in 2002 and finally to 73.5 per cent in 2014. Our energy import bill, which was $9.2 billion in 2002, was raised to $55 billion in 2014, as a result of these policies that abandoned our domestic and particularly renewable resources. The increasing external dependency in energy also leads to the most important reasons for our current deficit. This is not sustainable, and the solution is to quickly develop our domestic and renewable resources, our domestic manufacturing industry, with environmentally friendly technologies, and increase our energy efficiency. One of our renewable resources, still in a position that hasn't been activated, is our geothermal resources. With current technologies, about 15 percent of our geothermal potential to produce 16 billion kilowatts of electricity has been activated. Although our domestic and renewable sources offer us a great deal of potential for energy independence, a series of unlawful and irresponsible practices in the process of developing and producing these resources are causing huge reactions in the environments where investments are being made. Because of practices that ignore the echo system and its local people, nature is being irreversibly destroyed and the lives and health of those living in the region are at risk. The bag is not required by law to assess the environmental impact (SED)." While its application was almost made a rule, the CED reports became a form of non-violence by cutting-and-making method. Our geothermal resources, a very important economic resource and employment potential for our country, are also victims of these insensitive and purely commercial-interested policies. As a matter of fact, the Enlightenment Medical Office has brought this danger to the public's attention in a press release issued on November 19, 2015. The Enlightenment Medical Office is located in the region of Aydın, where geothermal energy investments are concentrated. Olives, chestnuts, figs suggest significant reductions in cotton resolves. According to the Enlightenment Medical Room, the production of dried figs has dropped by 50 per cent over the last year, and the olives have dropped by 23 per cent. On the other hand, there are serious increases in respiratory and circulatory problems and cancer cases. The number of cancer cases in Aydın province during 2010-2013 is 2.5 times higher than the average in Turkey, allegedly. The practice that victimized the local people has reached its climax with the lack of recognition of judicial decisions. In a lawsuit launched by Aydın Metropolitan Municipality against the opening of 36 new geothermal fields, the tender was cancelled in July 2015. However, these activities are being carried out this time with urgent decision to state. On November 9, 2015, the Council of Ministers decided to immediately nationalize the 17100 square metres of agricultural land and wetlands in the district of Yavuzköy, Aydın Province, Turkey. These practices, which are lawless and purely commercial, will also prevent our future domestic and renewable resources from being properly activated. In accordance with the above statements, we would like to propose the opening of Parliamental research under Articles 104 and 105 of the NGOs of the TEMM Administration and Articles 104 and 105 of the Constitution, as to what measures must be taken to prevent harming the environment, agricultural areas and human health, whether these measures are taken or not to be used as environmentally-friendly energy. Turkey to the chairman of the Grand National Assembly We propose and propose the amendment of Article (2/434) of Article 20 of the Law Against the Law Against Some Laws (/517) and the Justice and Development Party's Deputy Chair, Bkent Turan and Çanakkale MP Ayhan Yaz, Kurıkkale MP Ramazan Can and 23 MPs; and the amendment of some Laws. Edmund Kuşoğlu Mahmut Tanal Seyit Torun Tulip Carbage Senal Sarıhan Musa Pine The following article has been added to the Law No. 20-4447. "Half-work grant after birth and adoption EK MADDE 5- Employees are paid half-work grants during half of the week's free leave given according to the second part of Article 74 of Law 4857. The grant period is half of the week's work period specified in Article 63 of Law 4857. The half-work grant is paid monthly from the Fund in the second month after the month of your relationship with the monthly bonus and service certificate. The daily amount of half-work grant after birth and adoption is the gross amount of minimum wage per day. In order to benefit from these provisions, the employee must be granted an unemployment insurance premium for at least 600 days in the last three years prior to the birth or adoption date, with half a day's notice from the end of her maternity leave for birth and adoption, as well as a half-work form, as stated in Article 4857 in Article 63 of the week's work period, and as of the end of her maternity leave for birth and adoption. In addition to circumstances, the deadline for the application is reduced from the total amount of time earned to receive a half-work grant after birth and adoption. These payments will not be subject to any taxes or cuts except for stamp tax. Those who receive temporary unemployment grants are not paid for half a work grant after birth and adoption during the continuation of their condition. Unwarranted payments made by the employer for giving false information and documents are collected from the employer, along with legal interest. These provisions are also applied to personnel who are subject to the status of the chests under Article 20 of Law 506. For the insurance holders under the first joke, the principal gain of Article 5510 stated in Article 82 is paid to Social Security from the Fund as much as the number of days after the payment of half-work grants for employees and employers according to Article 81 of the same Law (a) and (f)bents. Under Law 5510, the institution is under the obligation to pay premiums, but it is not considered a business or employer, and they do not report entry and exit. The procedure and the basis for the implementation of this clause are determined together by the Ministry of Finance, Social Security Agency, the Undersecretary of the Institution and Treasury."
